{"type":"api-request-blob","sessionId":"ead3cae9-0b68-4987-b0da-e9d5763f22e8","hash":"5db7cd7e1df723b80a15c03c7802dbe90631d47cc4299382d19b93bffe49c06c","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_012YfcBTApEBydNnttsZeghT","type":"tool_result","content":"\"\"\"Posts: Markdown body, Draft/Published status, author, immutable slug.\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport re\nimport sqlite3\nfrom dataclasses import dataclass\n\nDRAFT = \"draft\"\nPUBLISHED = \"published\"\n\n\n@dataclass(frozen=True)\nclass Post:\n    id: int\n    title: str\n    slug: str\n    body: str\n    status: str\n    author_id: int\n    author_email: str\n    created_at: float\n    updated_at: float\n\n\n_SELECT = \"\"\"\nSELECT p.*, u.email AS author_email\nFROM posts p JOIN users u ON u.id = p.author_id\n\"\"\"\n\n\ndef _row_to_post(row: sqlite3.Row) -> Post:\n    return Post(row[\"id\"], row[\"title\"], row[\"slug\"], row[\"body\"], row[\"status\"],\n                row[\"author_id\"], row[\"author_email\"], row[\"created_at\"], row[\"updated_at\"])\n\n\ndef slugify(title: str) -> str:\n    return re.sub(r\"[^a-z0-9]+\", \"-\", title.lower()).strip(\"-\") or \"post\"\n\n\ndef _unique_slug(conn: sqlite3.Connection, title: str) -> str:\n    base = slugify(title)\n    slug, n = base, 1\n    while conn.execute(\"SELECT 1 FROM posts WHERE slug = ?\", (slug,)).fetchone():\n        n += 1\n        slug = f\"{base}-{n}\"\n    return slug\n\n\ndef create_post(conn: sqlite3.Connection, title: str, body: str, author_id: int,\n                now: float) -> int:\n    \"\"\"New Posts are always Drafts.\"\"\"\n    with conn:\n        cur = conn.execute(\n            \"INSERT INTO posts (title, slug, body, status, author_id, created_at, updated_at)\"\n            \" VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)\",\n            (title, _unique_slug(conn, title), body, DRAFT, author_id, now, now),\n        )\n    return cur.lastrowid\n\n\ndef get_post(conn: sqlite3.Connection, post_id: int) -> Post | None:\n    row = conn.execute(_SELECT + \" WHERE p.id = ?\", (post_id,)).fetchone()\n    return _row_to_post(row) if row else None\n\n\ndef list_posts(conn: sqlite3.Connection, author_id: int | None = None) -> list[Post]:\n    \"\"\"Newest first. author_id limits the list to one User's Posts.\"\"\"\n    sql, args = _SELECT, ()\n    if author_id is not None:\n        sql, args = sql + \" WHERE p.author_id = ?\", (author_id,)\n    rows = conn.execute(sql + \" ORDER BY p.created_at DESC, p.id DESC\", args).fetchall()\n    return [_row_to_post(r) for r in rows]\n\n\ndef update_post(conn: sqlite3.Connection, post_id: int, title: str, body: str,\n                now: float, status: str, slug: str | None = None) -> None:\n    \"\"\"The slug changes only when a normalised `slug` is passed (Admin-only callers).\"\"\"\n    with conn:\n        conn.execute(\n            \"UPDATE posts SET title = ?, body = ?, status = ?, updated_at = ?,\"\n            \" slug = COALESCE(?, slug) WHERE id = ?\",\n            (title, body, status, now, slug, post_id),\n        )\n\n\ndef set_status(conn: sqlite3.Connection, post_id: int, status: str, now: float) -> None:\n    with conn:\n        conn.execute(\"UPDATE posts SET status = ?, updated_at = ? WHERE id = ?\",\n                     (status, now, post_id))\n\n\ndef slug_taken(conn: sqlite3.Connection, slug: str, post_id: int) -> bool:\n    return conn.execute(\"SELECT 1 FROM posts WHERE slug = ? AND id != ?\",\n                        (slug, post_id)).fetchone() is not None\n\n\ndef delete_post(conn: sqlite3.Connection, post_id: int) -> None:\n    with conn:\n        conn.execute(\"DELETE FROM posts WHERE id = ?\", (post_id,))\n\"\"\"The admin console's home page and the /admin catch-all.\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nfrom fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, HTTPException, Request\n\nfrom app import auth\nfrom app.templating import templates\n\nrouter = APIRouter(prefix=\"/admin\")\n\n# Include this router LAST: an unknown /admin address still sends anonymous\n# visitors to the login page instead of a bare 404.\ncatch_all = APIRouter(prefix=\"/admin\")\n\n\n@router.get(\"\")\ndef admin_home(request: Request, user=Depends(auth.require_user)):\n    return templates.TemplateResponse(\n        request, \"admin/hello.html\", {\"title\": \"Admin\", \"user\": user}\n    )\n\n\n@catch_all.api_route(\"/{rest:path}\", include_in_schema=False,\n                     methods=[\"GET\", \"POST\", \"PUT\", \"PATCH\", \"DELETE\"],\n                     dependencies=[Depends(auth.require_user)])\ndef admin_unknown(rest: str):\n    raise HTTPException(status_code=404)\n\"\"\"Console routes for Posts: list, create, edit, preview, delete.\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nfrom fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, Form, HTTPException, Request\nfrom fastapi.responses import RedirectResponse\n\nfrom app import auth, db, posts\nfrom app.markdown import render_markdown\nfrom app.templating import templates\n\nrouter = APIRouter(prefix=\"/admin/posts\")\n\nNOT_YOURS = \"You can only open your own Posts\"\n\n\ndef _own_post(conn, post_id: int, user) -> posts.Post:\n    \"\"\"The Post, or a refusal. Editors reach only their own; Admins reach all.\"\"\"\n    post = posts.get_post(conn, post_id)\n    if post is None:\n        # An Editor gets the same refusal as for someone else's Post, so ids do not leak.\n        if user.role != \"admin\":\n            raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail=NOT_YOURS)\n        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail=\"No such Post\")\n    if user.role != \"admin\" and post.author_id != user.id:\n        raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail=NOT_YOURS)\n    return post\n\n\ndef _editor_page(request, user, post, title, body, error=None, status_code=200):\n    return templates.TemplateResponse(\n        request, \"admin/post_edit.html\",\n        {\"title\": \"Edit Post\" if post else \"New Post\", \"user\": user, \"post\": post,\n         \"form_title\": title, \"form_body\": body, \"preview_html\": render_markdown(body),\n         \"error\": error},\n        status_code=status_code,\n    )\n\n\n@router.get(\"\")\ndef post_list(request: Request, user=Depends(auth.require_user), conn=Depends(db.get_db)):\n    own_only = None if user.role == \"admin\" else user.id\n    return templates.TemplateResponse(\n        request, \"admin/post_list.html\",\n        {\"title\": \"Posts\", \"user\": user, \"posts\": posts.list_posts(conn, own_only)},\n    )\n\n\n@router.get(\"/new\")\ndef post_new(request: Request, user=Depends(auth.require_user)):\n    return _editor_page(request, user, None, \"\", \"\")\n\n\n@router.post(\"\")\ndef post_create(request: Request, title: str = Form(\"\"), body: str = Form(\"\"),\n                user=Depends(auth.require_user), conn=Depends(db.get_db)):\n    title = title.strip()\n    if not title:\n        return _editor_page(request, user, None, title, body, \"A Post needs a title\", 400)\n    post_id = posts.create_post(conn, title, body, user.id, auth.now(request))\n    return RedirectResponse(f\"/admin/posts/{post_id}\", status_code=303)\n\n\n@router.post(\"/preview\")\ndef post_preview(request: Request, title: str = Form(\"\"), body: str = Form(\"\"),\n                 post_id: int | None = Form(None),\n                 user=Depends(auth.require_user), conn=Depends(db.get_db)):\n    post = _own_post(conn, post_id, user) if post_id else None\n    return _editor_page(request, user, post, title, body)\n\n\n@router.get(\"/{post_id}\")\ndef post_edit(post_id: int, request: Request, user=Depends(auth.require_user),\n              conn=Depends(db.get_db)):\n    post = _own_post(conn, post_id, user)\n    return _editor_page(request, user, post, post.title, post.body)\n\n\n@router.post(\"/{post_id}\")\ndef post_update(post_id: int, request: Request, title: str = Form(\"\"), body: str = Form(\"\"),\n                slug: str | None = Form(None), user=Depends(auth.require_user), conn=Depends(db.get_db)):\n    post = _own_post(conn, post_id, user)\n    title = title.strip()\n    if not title:\n        return _editor_page(request, user, post, title, body, \"A Post needs a title\", 400)\n    new_slug = None\n    if user.role == \"admin\" and slug is not None and slug.strip():\n        new_slug = posts.slugify(slug)\n        if posts.slug_taken(conn, new_slug, post_id):\n            return _editor_page(request, user, post, title, body,\n                                f\"The slug “{new_slug}” is already used by another Post\", 400)\n    # An Editor's edit sends a Published Post back to Draft; an Admin's leaves it alone.\n    status = post.status if user.role == \"admin\" else posts.DRAFT\n    posts.update_post(conn, post_id, title, body, auth.now(request), status, new_slug)\n    return RedirectResponse(f\"/admin/posts/{post_id}\", status_code=303)\n\n\ndef _deletable(post: posts.Post, user) -> posts.Post:\n    if user.role != \"admin\" and post.status != posts.DRAFT:\n        raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail=\"Only a President can delete a Published Post\")\n    return post\n\n\n@router.get(\"/{post_id}/delete\")\ndef post_delete_confirm(post_id: int, request: Request, user=Depends(auth.require_user),\n                        conn=Depends(db.get_db)):\n    post = _deletable(_own_post(conn, post_id, user), user)\n    return templates.TemplateResponse(\n        request, \"admin/post_delete.html\", {\"title\": \"Delete Post\", \"user\": user, \"post\": post}\n    )\n\n\n@router.post(\"/{post_id}/delete\")\ndef post_delete(post_id: int, user=Depends(auth.require_user), conn=Depends(db.get_db)):\n    post = _deletable(_own_post(conn, post_id, user), user)\n    posts.delete_post(conn, post.id)\n    return RedirectResponse(\"/admin/posts\", status_code=303)\n\n\nCONFIRMS = {\n    \"publish\": (\"Publish this?\", \"Yes, publish it\", posts.DRAFT, posts.PUBLISHED,\n                \"It becomes Live at the next Publish run.\"),\n    \"unpublish\": (\"Are you sure you want to unpublish this?\", \"Yes, unpublish it\",\n                  posts.PUBLISHED, posts.DRAFT,\n                  \"It returns to Draft and leaves the public site at the next Publish run.\"),\n}\n\nadmin_only = Depends(auth.require_role(\"admin\"))\n\n\ndef _confirm_page(action: str, post_id: int, request, user, conn):\n    heading, button, _, _, note = CONFIRMS[action]\n    post = _own_post(conn, post_id, user)\n    return templates.TemplateResponse(\n        request, \"admin/post_confirm.html\",\n        {\"title\": heading, \"user\": user, \"post\": post, \"action\": action,\n         \"heading\": heading, \"button\": button, \"note\": note},\n    )\n\n\ndef _change_status(action: str, post_id: int, request, user, conn):\n    _, _, from_status, to_status, _ = CONFIRMS[action]\n    post = _own_post(conn, post_id, user)\n    if post.status == from_status:\n        posts.set_status(conn, post_id, to_status, auth.now(request))\n    return RedirectResponse(f\"/admin/posts/{post_id}\", status_code=303)\n\n\n@router.get(\"/{post_id}/publish\")\ndef post_publish_confirm(post_id: int, request: Request, user=admin_only,\n                         conn=Depends(db.get_db)):\n    return _confirm_page(\"publish\", post_id, request, user, conn)\n\n\n@router.post(\"/{post_id}/publish\")\ndef post_publish(post_id: int, request: Request, user=admin_only, conn=Depends(db.get_db)):\n    return _change_status(\"publish\", post_id, request, user, conn)\n\n\n@router.get(\"/{post_id}/unpublish\")\ndef post_unpublish_confirm(post_id: int, request: Request, user=admin_only,\n                           conn=Depends(db.get_db)):\n    return _confirm_page(\"unpublish\", post_id, request, user, conn)\n\n\n@router.post(\"/{post_id}/unpublish\")\ndef post_unpublish(post_id: int, request: Request, user=admin_only, conn=Depends(db.get_db)):\n    return _change_status(\"unpublish\", post_id, request, user, conn)\n\"\"\"SQLite access. One short-lived connection per request.\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport sqlite3\nfrom pathlib import Path\nfrom typing import Iterator\n\nfrom fastapi import Request\n\nSCHEMA = \"\"\"\nCREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS users (\n    id            INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,\n    email         TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE,\n    password_hash TEXT NOT NULL,\n    role          TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (role IN ('admin', 'editor')),\n    active        INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 1\n);\n\nCREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS posts (\n    id         INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,\n    title      TEXT NOT NULL,\n    slug       TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE,\n    body       TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '',\n    status     TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'draft' CHECK (status IN ('draft', 'published')),\n    author_id  INTEGER NOT NULL REFERENCES users (id),\n    created_at REAL NOT NULL,\n    updated_at REAL NOT NULL\n);\n\"\"\"\n\n\ndef connect(path: Path) -> sqlite3.Connection:\n    conn = sqlite3.connect(path, check_same_thread=False)\n    conn.row_factory = sqlite3.Row\n    return conn\n\n\ndef init_db(path: Path) -> None:\n    conn = connect(path)\n    with conn:\n        conn.executescript(SCHEMA)\n    conn.close()\n\n\ndef get_db(request: Request) -> Iterator[sqlite3.Connection]:\n    \"\"\"FastAPI dependency: a connection for this request, closed afterwards.\"\"\"\n    conn = connect(request.app.state.database)\n    try:\n        yield conn\n    finally:\n        conn.close()\n\"\"\"Users: argon2-hashed passwords, roles, the active flag.\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport sqlite3\nfrom dataclasses import dataclass\nfrom functools import cache\n\nfrom argon2 import PasswordHasher\nfrom argon2.exceptions import InvalidHashError, VerificationError\n\n_hasher = PasswordHasher()\n\n\n@dataclass(frozen=True)\nclass User:\n    id: int\n    email: str\n    role: str  # \"admin\" (President) or \"editor\" (Officer)\n    active: bool\n\n\ndef normalize_email(email: str) -> str:\n    return email.strip().lower()\n\n\ndef _row_to_user(row: sqlite3.Row) -> User:\n    return User(row[\"id\"], row[\"email\"], row[\"role\"], bool(row[\"active\"]))\n\n\ndef create_user(conn: sqlite3.Connection, email: str, password: str, role: str) -> None:\n    with conn:\n        conn.execute(\n            \"INSERT INTO users (email, password_hash, role) VALUES (?, ?, ?)\",\n            (normalize_email(email), _hasher.hash(password), role),\n        )\n\n\ndef get_user(conn: sqlite3.Connection, user_id: int) -> User | None:\n    row = conn.execute(\"SELECT * FROM users WHERE id = ?\", (user_id,)).fetchone()\n    return _row_to_user(row) if row else None\n\n\ndef email_exists(conn: sqlite3.Connection, email: str) -> bool:\n    return conn.execute(\n        \"SELECT 1 FROM users WHERE email = ?\", (normalize_email(email),)\n    ).fetchone() is not None\n\n\n@cache\ndef _dummy_hash() -> str:\n    return _hasher.hash(\"not-a-real-password\")\n\n\ndef authenticate(conn: sqlite3.Connection, email: str, password: str) -> User | None:\n    \"\"\"Return the User for a correct email and password, else None.\n\n    Unknown email, wrong password and Deactivated all look the same to the\n    caller, and unknown emails still cost one hash check so timing does not\n    reveal which accounts exist.\n    \"\"\"\n    row = conn.execute(\n        \"SELECT * FROM users WHERE email = ?\", (normalize_email(email),)\n    ).fetchone()\n    stored = row[\"password_hash\"] if row else _dummy_hash()\n    try:\n        _hasher.verify(stored, password)\n    except (VerificationError, InvalidHashError):\n        return None\n    if row is None or not row[\"active\"]:\n        return None\n    return _row_to_user(row)\n{% extends \"base.html\" %}\n{% block content %}\n  <h1>Hello admin</h1>\n  <p>Signed in as {{ user.email }} ({{ user.role }}).</p>\n  <p><a href=\"/admin/posts/new\">New Post</a> · <a href=\"/admin/posts\">Posts</a></p>\n  <form method=\"post\" action=\"/logout\">\n    <input type=\"hidden\" name=\"csrf_token\" value=\"{{ csrf_token() }}\">\n    <button type=\"submit\">Sign out</button>\n  </form>\n{% endblock %}\n{% extends \"base.html\" %}\n{% block content %}\n  <h1>Posts</h1>\n  <p><a href=\"/admin/posts/new\">New Post</a> · <a href=\"/admin\">Dashboard</a></p>\n  {% if posts %}\n  <ul>\n    {% for post in posts %}\n    <li>\n      <a href=\"/admin/posts/{{ post.id }}\">{{ post.title }}</a>\n      — {{ post.status | capitalize }}, by {{ post.author_email }}\n    </li>\n    {% endfor %}\n  </ul>\n  {% else %}\n  <p>No Posts yet.</p>\n  {% endif %}\n{% endblock %}\n\"\"\"Shared test fixtures.\n\n`client` gives you the app. `client_as(role)` gives you a client that is logged\nin as a seeded user of that role — it works as soon as your login route exists,\nso access-control tests stay one line:\n\n    def test_editor_cannot_manage_users(client_as):\n        assert client_as(\"editor\").get(\"/admin/users\").status_code in (302, 403)\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport re\n\nimport pytest\nfrom fastapi.testclient import TestClient\n\nfrom app.main import create_app\n\n# Matches scripts/seed_demo.py. Passwords come from the environment there; in\n# tests they are fixed and meaningless.\nDEMO_USERS = {\n    \"admin\": {\"email\": \"admin@example.test\", \"password\": \"test-admin-pw\"},\n    \"editor\": {\"email\": \"editor@example.test\", \"password\": \"test-editor-pw\"},\n}\n\n\nclass FakeClock:\n    \"\"\"Injected clock so idle sign-out is tested without waiting.\"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self, now: float = 1_000_000.0):\n        self.now = now\n\n    def __call__(self) -> float:\n        return self.now\n\n    def advance(self, seconds: float) -> None:\n        self.now += seconds\n\n\n@pytest.fixture\ndef clock() -> FakeClock:\n    return FakeClock()\n\n\n@pytest.fixture\ndef make_app(tmp_path, clock):\n    \"\"\"Factory for a fresh app on its own seeded database and the fake clock.\"\"\"\n    from app.seed import seed_users\n\n    def _make():\n        db = tmp_path / \"test.db\"\n        application = create_app(database=db, clock=clock)\n        seed_users(db, {role: (u[\"email\"], u[\"password\"])\n                        for role, u in DEMO_USERS.items()})\n        return application\n\n    return _make\n\n\n@pytest.fixture\ndef client(make_app) -> TestClient:\n    return TestClient(make_app())\n\n\ndef csrf_of(client: TestClient, path: str = \"/login\") -> str:\n    \"\"\"Read the CSRF token out of the form on `path`.\"\"\"\n    html = client.get(path).text\n    return re.search(r'name=\"csrf_token\" value=\"([^\"]+)\"', html).group(1)\n\n\ndef sign_in(client: TestClient, email: str, password: str, token: str | None = None):\n    return client.post(\n        \"/login\",\n        data={\"email\": email, \"password\": password,\n              \"csrf_token\": token or csrf_of(client)},\n        follow_redirects=False,\n    )\n\n\n@pytest.fixture\ndef client_as(make_app):\n    \"\"\"Return a factory: client_as(\"editor\") -> a logged-in TestClient.\"\"\"\n\n    def _login(role: str) -> TestClient:\n        user = DEMO_USERS[role]\n        c = TestClient(make_app())\n        response = sign_in(c, user[\"email\"], user[\"password\"])\n        assert response.status_code in (200, 302, 303), (\n            f\"Login as {role} failed with {response.status_code}\")\n        return c\n\n    return _login\n\"\"\"T03: Presidents publish, unpublish and delete Posts. Seam 1: the HTTP app.\"\"\"\nfrom fastapi.testclient import TestClient\n\nfrom tests.conftest import csrf_of, sign_in\nfrom tests.test_t02_posts import create_post, post_url\n\n\ndef president_for(editor) -> TestClient:\n    \"\"\"A President on the same app and database as `editor`.\"\"\"\n    admin = TestClient(editor.app)\n    sign_in(admin, \"admin@example.test\", \"test-admin-pw\")\n    return admin\n\n\ndef act(c, url, action, **extra):\n    token = csrf_of(c, url)\n    return c.post(f\"{url}/{action}\", data={\"csrf_token\": token, **extra},\n                  follow_redirects=False)\n\n\ndef published_post(editor, title=\"Pitch: NVDA\"):\n    url = post_url(create_post(editor, title=title))\n    admin = president_for(editor)\n    assert act(admin, url, \"publish\").status_code == 303\n    assert \"Published\" in admin.get(url).text\n    return url, admin\n\n\ndef test_president_publishes_a_draft_after_a_confirm(client_as):\n    editor = client_as(\"editor\")\n    url = post_url(create_post(editor))\n    admin = president_for(editor)\n    confirm = admin.get(url + \"/publish\")\n    assert confirm.status_code == 200\n    assert \"Publish this?\" in confirm.text\n    assert 'name=\"csrf_token\"' in confirm.text\n    assert \"Status: Draft\" in admin.get(url).text  # a GET publishes nothing\n    assert act(admin, url, \"publish\").status_code == 303\n    assert \"Status: Published\" in admin.get(url).text\n\n\ndef test_officer_cannot_publish_even_by_direct_post(client_as):\n    editor = client_as(\"editor\")\n    url = post_url(create_post(editor))\n    assert editor.get(url + \"/publish\").status_code == 403\n    assert act(editor, url, \"publish\").status_code == 403\n    assert \"Status: Draft\" in editor.get(url).text\n\n\ndef test_president_unpublishes_a_published_post(client_as):","is_error":false}]}}

{"parentUuid":"c4acf382-c70d-4260-a446-9db12b715fd4","isSidechain":false,"promptId":"cb145c49-8cdc-415f-98a5-dd675de1cd2b","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_012YfcBTApEBydNnttsZeghT","type":"tool_result","content":"\"\"\"Posts: Markdown body, Draft/Published status, author, immutable slug.\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport re\nimport sqlite3\nfrom dataclasses import dataclass\n\nDRAFT = \"draft\"\nPUBLISHED = \"published\"\n\n\n@dataclass(frozen=True)\nclass Post:\n    id: int\n    title: str\n    slug: str\n    body: str\n    status: str\n    author_id: int\n    author_email: str\n    created_at: float\n    updated_at: float\n\n\n_SELECT = \"\"\"\nSELECT p.*, u.email AS author_email\nFROM posts p JOIN users u ON u.id = p.author_id\n\"\"\"\n\n\ndef _row_to_post(row: sqlite3.Row) -> Post:\n    return Post(row[\"id\"], row[\"title\"], row[\"slug\"], row[\"body\"], row[\"status\"],\n                row[\"author_id\"], row[\"author_email\"], row[\"created_at\"], row[\"updated_at\"])\n\n\ndef slugify(title: str) -> str:\n    return re.sub(r\"[^a-z0-9]+\", \"-\", title.lower()).strip(\"-\") or \"post\"\n\n\ndef _unique_slug(conn: sqlite3.Connection, title: str) -> str:\n    base = slugify(title)\n    slug, n = base, 1\n    while conn.execute(\"SELECT 1 FROM posts WHERE slug = ?\", (slug,)).fetchone():\n        n += 1\n        slug = f\"{base}-{n}\"\n    return slug\n\n\ndef create_post(conn: sqlite3.Connection, title: str, body: str, author_id: int,\n                now: float) -> int:\n    \"\"\"New Posts are always Drafts.\"\"\"\n    with conn:\n        cur = conn.execute(\n            \"INSERT INTO posts (title, slug, body, status, author_id, created_at, updated_at)\"\n            \" VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)\",\n            (title, _unique_slug(conn, title), body, DRAFT, author_id, now, now),\n        )\n    return cur.lastrowid\n\n\ndef get_post(conn: sqlite3.Connection, post_id: int) -> Post | None:\n    row = conn.execute(_SELECT + \" WHERE p.id = ?\", (post_id,)).fetchone()\n    return _row_to_post(row) if row else None\n\n\ndef list_posts(conn: sqlite3.Connection, author_id: int | None = None) -> list[Post]:\n    \"\"\"Newest first. author_id limits the list to one User's Posts.\"\"\"\n    sql, args = _SELECT, ()\n    if author_id is not None:\n        sql, args = sql + \" WHERE p.author_id = ?\", (author_id,)\n    rows = conn.execute(sql + \" ORDER BY p.created_at DESC, p.id DESC\", args).fetchall()\n    return [_row_to_post(r) for r in rows]\n\n\ndef update_post(conn: sqlite3.Connection, post_id: int, title: str, body: str,\n                now: float, status: str, slug: str | None = None) -> None:\n    \"\"\"The slug changes only when a normalised `slug` is passed (Admin-only callers).\"\"\"\n    with conn:\n        conn.execute(\n            \"UPDATE posts SET title = ?, body = ?, status = ?, updated_at = ?,\"\n            \" slug = COALESCE(?, slug) WHERE id = ?\",\n            (title, body, status, now, slug, post_id),\n        )\n\n\ndef set_status(conn: sqlite3.Connection, post_id: int, status: str, now: float) -> None:\n    with conn:\n        conn.execute(\"UPDATE posts SET status = ?, updated_at = ? WHERE id = ?\",\n                     (status, now, post_id))\n\n\ndef slug_taken(conn: sqlite3.Connection, slug: str, post_id: int) -> bool:\n    return conn.execute(\"SELECT 1 FROM posts WHERE slug = ? None.\n\n    Unknown email, wrong password and Deactivated all look the same to the\n    caller, and unknown emails still cost one hash check so timing does not\n    reveal which accounts exist.\n templates.TemplateResponse(\n        request, \"admin/post_list.html\",\n        {\"title\": \"Posts\", \"user\": user, \"posts\": posts.list_posts(conn, own_only)},\n    )\n\n\n@router.get(\"/new\")\ndef post_new(request: Request, user=Depends(auth.require_user)):\n    return _editor_page(request, user, None, \"\", \"\")\n\n\n@router.post(\"\")\ndef post_create(request: Request, title: str = Form(\"\"), body: str = Form(\"\"),\n                user=Depends(auth.require_user), conn=Depends(db.get_db)):\n    title = title.strip()\n    if not title:\n        return _editor_page(request, user, None, title, body, \"A Post needs a title\", 400)\n    post_id = posts.create_post(conn, title, body, user.id, auth.now(request))\n    return RedirectResponse(f\"/admin/posts/{post_id}\", status_code=303)\n\n\n@router.post(\"/preview\")\ndef post_preview(request: Request, title: str = Form(\"\"), body: str = Form(\"\"),\n                 post_id: int | None = Form(None),\n                 user=Depends(auth.require_user), conn=Depends(db.get_db)):\n    post = _own_post(conn, post_id, user) if post_id else None\n    return _editor_page(request, user, post, title, body)\n\n\n@router.get(\"/{post_id}\")\ndef post_edit(post_id: int, request: Request, user=Depends(auth.require_user),\n              conn=Depends(db.get_db)):\n    post = _own_post(conn, post_id, user)\n    return _editor_page(request, user, post, post.title, post.body)\n\n\n@router.post(\"/{post_id}\")\ndef post_update(post_id: int, request: Request, title: str = Form(\"\"), body: str = Form(\"\"),\n                slug: str | None = Form(None), user=Depends(auth.require_user), conn=Depends(db.get_db)):\n    post = _own_post(conn, post_id, user)\n    title = title.strip()\n    if not title:\n        return _editor_page(request, user, post, title, body, \"A Post needs a title\", 400)\n    new_slug = None\n    if user.role == \"admin\" and slug is not None and slug.strip():\n        new_slug = posts.slugify(slug)\n        if posts.slug_taken(conn, new_slug, post_id):\n            return _editor_page(request, user, post, title, body,\n                                f\"The slug “{new_slug}” is already used by another Post\", 400)\n    # An Editor's edit sends a Published Post back to Draft; an Admin's leaves it alone.\n    status = post.status if user.role == \"admin\" else posts.DRAFT\n    posts.update_post(conn, post_id, title, body, auth.now(request), status, new_slug)\n    return RedirectResponse(f\"/admin/posts/{post_id}\", status_code=303)\n\n\ndef _deletable(post: posts.Post, user) -> posts.Post:\n    if user.role != \"admin\" and post.status != posts.DRAFT:\n        raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail=\"Only a President can delete a Published Post\")\n    return post\n\n\n@router.get(\"/{post_id}/delete\")\ndef post_delete_confirm(post_id: int, request: Request, user=Depends(auth.require_user),\n                        conn=Depends(db.get_db)):\n    post = _deletable(_own_post(conn, post_id, user), user)\n    return templates.TemplateResponse(\n        request, \"admin/post_delete.html\", {\"title\": \"Delete Post\", \"user\": user, \"post\": post}\n    )\n\n\n@router.post(\"/{post_id}/delete\")\ndef post_delete(post_id: int, user=Depends(auth.require_user), conn=Depends(db.get_db)):\n    post = _deletable(_own_post(conn, post_id, user), user)\n    posts.delete_post(conn, post.id)\n    return RedirectResponse(\"/admin/posts\", status_code=303)\n\n\nCONFIRMS = {\n    \"publish\": (\"Publish this?\", \"Yes, publish it\", posts.DRAFT, posts.PUBLISHED,\n                \"It becomes Live at the next Publish run.\"),\n    \"unpublish\": (\"Are you sure you want to unpublish this?\", \"Yes, unpublish it\",\n                  posts.PUBLISHED, posts.DRAFT,\n                  \"It returns to Draft and leaves the public site at the next Publish run.\"),\n}\n\nadmin_only = Depends(auth.require_role(\"admin\"))\n\n\ndef _confirm_page(action: str, post_id: int, request, user, conn):\n    heading, button, _, _, note = CONFIRMS[action]\n    post = _own_post(conn, post_id, user)\n    return templates.TemplateResponse(\n        request, \"admin/post_confirm.html\",\n        {\"title\": heading, \"user\": user, \"post\": post, \"action\": action,\n         \"heading\": heading, \"button\": button, \"note\": note},\n    )\n\n\ndef _change_status(action: str, post_id: int, request, user, conn):\n    _, _, from_status, to_status, _ = CONFIRMS[action]\n    post = _own_post(conn, post_id, user)\n    if post.status == from_status:\n        posts.set_status(conn, post_id, to_status, auth.now(request))\n    return RedirectResponse(f\"/admin/posts/{post_id}\", status_code=303)\n\n\n@router.get(\"/{post_id}/publish\")\ndef post_publish_confirm(post_id: int, request: Request, user=admin_only,\n                         conn=Depends(db.get_db)):\n    return _confirm_page(\"publish\", post_id, request, user, conn)\n\n\n@router.post(\"/{post_id}/publish\")\ndef post_publish(post_id: int, request: Request, user=admin_only, conn=Depends(db.get_db)):\n    return _change_status(\"publish\", post_id, request, user, conn)\n\n\n@router.get(\"/{post_id}/unpublish\")\ndef post_unpublish_confirm(post_id: int, request: Request, user=admin_only,\n                           conn=Depends(db.get_db)):\n    return _confirm_page(\"unpublish\", post_id, request, user, conn)\n\n\n@router.post(\"/{post_id}/unpublish\")\ndef post_unpublish(post_id: int, request: Request, user=admin_only, conn=Depends(db.get_db)):\n    return _change_status(\"unpublish\", post_id, request, user, conn)\n\"\"\"SQLite access. One short-lived connection per request.\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport sqlite3\nfrom pathlib import Path\nfrom typing import Iterator\n\nfrom fastapi import Request\n\nSCHEMA = \"\"\"\nCREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS users (\n    id            INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,\n    email         TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE,\n    password_hash TEXT NOT NULL,\n    role          TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (role IN ('admin', 'editor')),\n    active        INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 1\n);\n\nCREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS posts (\n    id         INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,\n    title      TEXT NOT NULL,\n    slug       TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE,\n    body       TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '',\n    status     TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'draft' CHECK (status IN ('draft', 'published')),\n    author_id  INTEGER NOT NULL REFERENCES users (id),\n    created_at REAL NOT NULL,\n    updated_at REAL NOT NULL\n);\n\"\"\"\n\n\ndef connect(path: Path) -> sqlite3.Connection:\n    conn = sqlite3.connect(path, check_same_thread=False)\n    conn.row_factory = sqlite3.Row\n    return conn\n\n\ndef init_db(path: Path) -> None:\n    conn = connect(path)\n    with conn:\n        conn.executescript(SCHEMA)\n    conn.close()\n\n\ndef get_db(request: Request) -> Iterator[sqlite3.Connection]:\n    \"\"\"FastAPI dependency: a connection for this request, closed afterwards.\"\"\"\n    conn = connect(request.app.state.database)\n    try:\n        yield conn\n    finally:\n        conn.close()\n\"\"\"Users: argon2-hashed passwords, roles, the active flag.\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport sqlite3\nfrom dataclasses import dataclass\nfrom functools import cache\n\nfrom argon2 import PasswordHasher\nfrom argon2.exceptions import InvalidHashError, VerificationError\n\n_hasher = PasswordHasher()\n\n\n@dataclass(frozen=True)\nclass User:\n    id: int\n    email: str\n    role: str  # \"admin\" (President) or \"editor\" (Officer)\n    active: bool\n\n\ndef normalize_email(email: str) -> str:\n    return email.strip().lower()\n\n\ndef _row_to_user(row: sqlite3.Row) -> User:\n    return User(row[\"id\"], row[\"email\"], row[\"role\"], bool(row[\"active\"]))\n\n\ndef create_user(conn: sqlite3.Connection, email: str, password: str, role: str) -> None:\n    with conn:\n        conn.execute(\n            \"INSERT INTO users (email, password_hash, role) VALUES (?, ?, ?)\",\n            (normalize_email(email), _hasher.hash(password), role),\n        )\n\n\ndef get_user(conn: sqlite3.Connection, user_id: int) -> User | None:\n    row = conn.execute(\"SELECT * FROM users WHERE id = ?\", (user_id,)).fetchone()\n    return _row_to_user(row) if row else None\n\n\ndef email_exists(conn: sqlite3.Connection, email: str) -> bool:\n    return conn.execute(\n        \"SELECT 1 FROM users WHERE email = ?\", (normalize_email(email),)\n    ).fetchone() is not None\n\n\n@cache\ndef _dummy_hash() -> str:\n    return _hasher.hash(\"not-a-real-password\")\n\n\ndef authenticate(conn: sqlite3.Connection, email: str, password: str) -> User | None:\n    \"\"\"Return the User for a correct email and password, else None.\n\n    Unknown email, wrong password and Deactivated all look the same to the\n    caller, and unknown emails still cost one hash check so timing does not\n    reveal which accounts exist.\n    \"\"\"\n    row = conn.execute(\n        \"SELECT * FROM users WHERE email = ?\", (normalize_email(email),)\n    ).fetchone()\n    stored = row[\"password_hash\"] if row else _dummy_hash()\n    try:\n        _hasher.verify(stored, password)\n    except (VerificationError, InvalidHashError):\n        return None\n    if row is None or not row[\"active\"]:\n        return None\n    return _row_to_user(row)\n{% extends \"base.html\" %}\n{% block content %}\n  <h1>Hello admin</h1>\n  <p>Signed in as {{ user.email }} ({{ user.role }}).</p>\n  <p><a href=\"/admin/posts/new\">New Post</a> · <a href=\"/admin/posts\">Posts</a></p>\n  <form method=\"post\" action=\"/logout\">\n    <input type=\"hidden\" name=\"csrf_token\" value=\"{{ csrf_token() }}\">\n    <button type=\"submit\">Sign out</button>\n  </form>\n{% endblock %}\n{% extends \"base.html\" %}\n{% block content %}\n  <h1>Posts</h1>\n  <p><a href=\"/admin/posts/new\">New Post</a> · <a href=\"/admin\">Dashboard</a></p>\n  {% if posts %}\n  <ul>\n    {% for post in posts %}\n    <li>\n      <a href=\"/admin/posts/{{ post.id }}\">{{ post.title }}</a>\n      — {{ post.status | capitalize }}, by {{ post.author_email }}\n    </li>\n    {% endfor %}\n  </ul>\n  {% else %}\n  <p>No Posts yet.</p>\n  {% endif %}\n{% endblock %}\n\"\"\"Shared test fixtures.\n\n`client` gives you the app. `client_as(role)` gives you a client that is logged\nin as a seeded user of that role — it works as soon as your login route exists,\nso access-control tests stay one line:\n\n    def test_editor_cannot_manage_users(client_as):\n        assert client_as(\"editor\").get(\"/admin/users\").status_code in (302, 403)\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport re\n\nimport pytest\nfrom fastapi.testclient import TestClient\n\nfrom app.main import create_app\n\n# Matches scripts/seed_demo.py. Passwords come from the environment there; in\n# tests they are fixed and meaningless.\nDEMO_USERS = {\n    \"admin\": {\"email\": \"admin@example.test\", \"password\": \"test-admin-pw\"},\n    \"editor\": {\"email\": \"editor@example.test\", \"password\": \"test-editor-pw\"},\n}\n\n\nclass FakeClock:\n    \"\"\"Injected clock so idle sign-out is tested without waiting.\"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self, now: float = 1_000_000.0):\n        self.now = now\n\n    def __call__(self) -> float:\n        return self.now\n\n    def advance(self, seconds: float) -> None:\n        self.now += seconds\n\n\n@pytest.fixture\ndef clock() -> FakeClock:\n    return FakeClock()\n\n\n@pytest.fixture\ndef make_app(tmp_path, clock):\n    \"\"\"Factory for a fresh app on its own seeded database and the fake clock.\"\"\"\n    from app.seed import seed_users\n\n    def _make():\n        db = tmp_path / \"test.db\"\n        application = create_app(database=db, clock=clock)\n        seed_users(db, {role: (u[\"email\"], u[\"password\"])\n                        for role, u in DEMO_USERS.items()})\n        return application\n\n    return _make\n\n\n@pytest.fixture\ndef client(make_app) -> TestClient:\n    return TestClient(make_app())\n\n\ndef csrf_of(client: TestClient, path: str = \"/login\") -> str:\n    \"\"\"Read the CSRF token out of the form on `path`.\"\"\"\n    html = client.get(path).text\n    return re.search(r'name=\"csrf_token\" value=\"([^\"]+)\"', html).group(1)\n\n\ndef sign_in(client: TestClient, email: str, password: str, token: str | None = None):\n    return client.post(\n        \"/login\",\n        data={\"email\": email, \"password\": password,\n              \"csrf_token\": token or csrf_of(client)},\n        follow_redirects=False,\n    )\n\n\n@pytest.fixture\ndef client_as(make_app):\n    \"\"\"Return a factory: client_as(\"editor\") -> a logged-in TestClient.\"\"\"\n\n    def _login(role: str) -> TestClient:\n        user = DEMO_USERS[role]\n        c = TestClient(make_app())\n        response = sign_in(c, user[\"email\"], user[\"password\"])\n        assert response.status_code in (200, 302, 303), (\n            f\"Login as {role} failed with {response.status_code}\")\n        return c\n\n    return _login\n\"\"\"T03: Presidents publish, unpublish and delete Posts. Seam 1: the HTTP app.\"\"\"\nfrom fastapi.testclient import TestClient\n\nfrom tests.conftest import csrf_of, sign_in\nfrom tests.test_t02_posts import create_post, post_url\n\n\ndef president_for(editor) -> TestClient:\n    \"\"\"A President on the same app and database as `editor`.\"\"\"\n    admin = TestClient(editor.app)\n    sign_in(admin, \"admin@example.test\", \"test-admin-pw\")\n    return admin\n\n\ndef act(c, url, action, **extra):\n    token = csrf_of(c, url)\n    return c.post(f\"{url}/{action}\", data={\"csrf_token\": token, **extra},\n                  follow_redirects=False)\n\n\ndef published_post(editor, title=\"Pitch: NVDA\"):\n    url = post_url(create_post(editor, title=title))\n    admin = president_for(editor)\n    assert act(admin, url, \"publish\").status_code == 303\n    assert \"Published\" in admin.get(url).text\n    return url, admin\n\n\ndef test_president_publishes_a_draft_after_a_confirm(client_as):\n    editor = client_as(\"editor\")\n    url = post_url(create_post(editor))\n    admin = president_for(editor)\n    confirm = admin.get(url + \"/publish\")\n    assert confirm.status_code == 200\n    assert \"Publish this?\" in confirm.text\n    assert 'name=\"csrf_token\"' in confirm.text\n    assert \"Status: Draft\" in admin.get(url).text  # a GET publishes nothing\n    assert act(admin, url, \"publish\").status_code == 303\n    assert \"Status: Published\" in admin.get(url).text\n\n\ndef test_officer_cannot_publish_even_by_direct_post(client_as):\n    editor = client_as(\"editor\")\n    url = post_url(create_post(editor))\n    assert editor.get(url + \"/publish\").status_code == 403\n    assert act(editor, url, \"publish\").status_code == 403\n    assert \"Status: Draft\" in editor.get(url).text\n\n\ndef test_president_unpublishes_a_published_post(client_as):","stderr":"","interrupted":false,"isImage":false,"noOutputExpected":false},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"c4acf382-c70d-4260-a446-9db12b715fd4","permissionDecision":{"decision":"accept","source":"config","reasonType":"mode"},"session_id":"ead3cae9-0b68-4987-b0da-e9d5763f22e8","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/biswas1/code/iphs400-mp2-cms","sessionId":"ead3cae9-0b68-4987-b0da-e9d5763f22e8","version":"2.1.300","gitBranch":"main"}

{"type":"api-request-blob","sessionId":"ead3cae9-0b68-4987-b0da-e9d5763f22e8","hash":"64149edd0cc3023e7f0d35cabc1550293b1992707cd4eaf704fca8236e3697c0","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01FTsvqudTpB5tH97fUZbM1y","type":"tool_result","content":"tests/conftest.py:8:        assert client_as(\"editor\").get(\"/admin/users\").status_code in (302, 403)\ntests/conftest.py:22:    \"admin\": {\"email\": \"admin@example.test\", \"password\": \"test-admin-pw\"},\ntests/test_t03_president_control.py:89:    assert \"Pitch: NVDA\" in admin.get(\"/admin/posts\").text\ntests/test_t03_president_control.py:92:    assert \"Pitch: NVDA\" not in admin.get(\"/admin/posts\").text\ntests/test_t03_president_control.py:102:    assert \"Their draft\" not in admin.get(\"/admin/posts\").text\ntests/test_t02_posts.py:8:    token = csrf_of(c, \"/admin/posts/new\")\ntests/test_t02_posts.py:9:    return c.post(\"/admin/posts\", data={\"title\": title, \"body\": body, \"csrf_token\": token},\ntests/test_t02_posts.py:21:    assert re.fullmatch(r\"/admin/posts/\\d+\", url)\ntests/test_t02_posts.py:26:    listing = c.get(\"/admin/posts\").text\ntests/test_t02_posts.py:72:    listing = mine.get(\"/admin/posts\").text\ntests/test_t02_posts.py:77:    token = csrf_of(mine, \"/admin/posts/new\")\ntests/test_t02_posts.py:91:    assert \"Pitch: NVDA\" in admin.get(\"/admin/posts\").text\ntests/test_t02_posts.py:118:    token = csrf_of(c, \"/admin/posts/new\")\ntests/test_t02_posts.py:119:    return c.post(\"/admin/posts/preview\",\ntests/test_t02_posts.py:151:    assert \"Abandoned idea\" in c.get(\"/admin/posts\").text  # a GET deletes nothing\ntests/test_t02_posts.py:155:    assert \"Abandoned idea\" not in c.get(\"/admin/posts\").text\ntests/test_t02_posts.py:162:    for path in (\"/admin/posts/new\", url, url + \"/delete\"):\ntests/test_t02_posts.py:164:    for path in (\"/admin/posts\", url, url + \"/delete\", \"/admin/posts/preview\"):\ntests/test_t02_posts.py:171:    assert \"/admin/posts\" in client_as(\"editor\").get(\"/admin\").text\ntests/test_t01_login.py:8:    assert response.headers[\"location\"] == \"/admin\"\n\"\"\"T02: Officers write and edit their own Post drafts. Seam 1: the HTTP app.\"\"\"\nimport re\n\nfrom tests.conftest import csrf_of\n\n\ndef create_post(c, title=\"Meeting announcement\", body=\"Come to **Oden 110**.\"):\n    token = csrf_of(c, \"/admin/posts/new\")\n    return c.post(\"/admin/posts\", data={\"title\": title, \"body\": body, \"csrf_token\": token},\n                  follow_redirects=False)\n\n\ndef post_url(response) -> str:\n    assert response.status_code == 303\n    return response.headers[\"location\"]\n\n\ndef test_officer_creates_a_draft_they_own(client_as):\n    c = client_as(\"editor\")\n    url = post_url(create_post(c))\n    assert re.fullmatch(r\"/admin/posts/\\d+\", url)\n    page = c.get(url)\n    assert page.status_code == 200\n    assert \"Meeting announcement\" in page.text\n    assert \"Draft\" in page.text\n    listing = c.get(\"/admin/posts\").text\n    assert \"Meeting announcement\" in listing\n    assert \"editor@example.test\" in listing\n\n\ndef test_slug_is_made_once_unique_and_survives_title_edits(client_as):\n    c = client_as(\"editor\")\n    first = post_url(create_post(c, title=\"Spring Stock Pitch!\"))\n    second = post_url(create_post(c, title=\"Spring Stock Pitch!\"))\n    assert \"spring-stock-pitch\" in c.get(first).text\n    assert \"spring-stock-pitch-2\" in c.get(second).text\n    token = csrf_of(c, first)\n    c.post(first, data={\"title\": \"Totally different\", \"body\": \"x\", \"csrf_token\": token})\n    edited = c.get(first).text\n    assert \"Totally different\" in edited\n<!doctype html>\n<html lang=\"en\">\n<head>\n  <meta charset=\"utf-8\">\n  <meta name=\"viewport\" content=\"width=device-width, initial-scale=1\">\n  <title>{% block title %}{{ title }}{% endblock %}</title>\n  <link rel=\"stylesheet\" href=\"{{ css_path | default('style.css') }}\">\n</head>\n<body>\n  <header><a href=\"{{ home_path | default('index.html') }}\">{{ title }}</a></header>\n  <main>{% block content %}{% endblock %}</main>\n  <footer><small>IPHS 400 Mini-Project #2</small></footer>\n</body>\n</html>\n\"\"\"Create the demo President and Officer. Safe to run repeatedly.\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nfrom app import db, users\n\n\ndef seed_users(database: Path, accounts: dict[str, tuple[str, str]]) -> list[str]:\n    \"\"\"accounts maps role -> (email, password). Existing emails are left alone.\n\n    Returns the emails that were created. Passwords are hashed by users.create_user\n    and are never printed or stored as text.\n    \"\"\"\n    db.init_db(database)\n    conn = db.connect(database)\n    created = []\n    for role, (email, password) in accounts.items():\n        if not users.email_exists(conn, email):\n            users.create_user(conn, email, password, role)\n            created.append(email)\n    conn.close()\n    return created","is_error":false}]}}

Seam 1: the HTTP app.\"\"\"\nimport re\n\nfrom tests.conftest import csrf_of\n\n\ndef create_post(c, title=\"Meeting announcement\", body=\"Come to **Oden 110**.\"):\n    token = csrf_of(c, \"/admin/posts/new\")\n    return c.post(\"/admin/posts\", data={\"title\": title, \"body\": body, \"csrf_token\": token},\n                  follow_redirects=False)\n\n\ndef post_url(response) -> str:\n    assert response.status_code == 303\n    return response.headers[\"location\"]\n\n\ndef test_officer_creates_a_draft_they_own(client_as):\n    c = client_as(\"editor\")\n    url = post_url(create_post(c))\n    assert re.fullmatch(r\"/admin/posts/\\d+\", url)\n    page = c.get(url)\n    assert page.status_code == 200\n    assert \"Meeting announcement\" in page.text\n    assert \"Draft\" in page.text\n    listing = c.get(\"/admin/posts\").text\n    assert \"Meeting announcement\" in listing\n    assert \"editor@example.test\" in listing\n\n\ndef test_slug_is_made_once_unique_and_survives_title_edits(client_as):\n    c = client_as(\"editor\")\n    first = post_url(create_post(c, title=\"Spring Stock Pitch!\"))\n    second = post_url(create_post(c, title=\"Spring Stock Pitch!\"))\n    assert \"spring-stock-pitch\" in c.get(first).text\n    assert \"spring-stock-pitch-2\" in c.get(second).text\n    token = csrf_of(c, first)\n    c.post(first, data={\"title\": \"Totally different\", \"body\": \"x\", \"csrf_token\": token})\n    edited = c.get(first).text\n    assert \"Totally different\" in edited\n<!doctype {"parentUuid":"bc5c6350-425f-4a2f-9ce7-8e52ee765f92","isSidechain":false,"message":{"model":"claude-sonnet-5-5","id":"msg_011Cg3LuzFu29wwSgRTVU7h9","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01HKE2NeuhtQRh2PGJMgjoiz","name":"Bash","input":{"command":"cat > tests/test_t04_dashboard.py <<'EOF'\n\"\"\"T04: Dashboard counts and the filterable Content list. Seam 1: the HTTP app.\"\"\"\nimport re\n\nfrom app import db\nfrom tests.test_t02_posts import create_post, post_url\nfrom tests.test_t03_president_control import act, president_for\n\n\ndef count_of(html: str, label: str) -> int:\n    \"\"\"The number printed next to a label such as 'Drafts: 3'.\"\"\"\n    return int(re.search(rf\"{label}:\\s*(\\d+)\", html).group(1))\n\n\ndef titles_in_order(html: str, titles: list[str]) -> list[str]:\n    return sorted((t for t in titles if t in html), key=html.index)\n\n\ndef test_dashboard_counts_follow_post_changes(client_as):\n    editor = client_as(\"editor\")\n    admin = president_for(editor)\n    assert count_of(admin.get(\"/admin\").text, \"Drafts\") == 0\n    url = post_url(create_post(editor, title=\"One\"))\n    create_post(editor, title=\"Two\")\n    page = admin.get(\"/admin\").text\n    assert (count_of(page, \"Drafts\"), count_of(page, \"Published\")) == (2, 0)\n    act(admin, url, \"publish\")\n    page = admin.get(\"/admin\").text\n    assert (count_of(page, \"Drafts\"), count_of(page, \"Published\")) == (1, 1)\n\n\ndef test_dashboard_has_a_new_post_button_that_opens_the_editor(client_as):\n    c = client_as(\"editor\")\n    page = c.get(\"/admin\").text\n    assert 'href=\"/admin/posts/new\"' in page\n    assert c.get(\"/admin/posts/new\").status_code == 200\n\n\ndef test_president_sees_user_counts_officer_does_not(client_as):\n    editor = client_as(\"editor\")\n    admin = president_for(editor)\n    page = admin.get(\"/admin\").text\n    assert (count_of(page, \"Active Users\"), count_of(page, \"Deactivated Users\")) == (2, 0)\n    conn = db.connect(editor.app.state.database)\n    with conn:\n        conn.execute(\"UPDATE users SET active = 0 WHERE role = 'editor'\")\n    conn.close()\n    page = admin.get(\"/admin\").text\n    assert (count_of(page, \"Active Users\"), count_of(page, \"Deactivated Users\")) == (1, 1)\n    assert \"Active Users\" not in editor.get(\"/admin\").text\n    assert \"Deactivated Users\" not in editor.get(\"/admin\").text\n\n\ndef test_content_list_is_newest_first_with_status_links_and_counts(client_as):\n    editor = client_as(\"editor\")\n    admin = president_for(editor)\n    old = post_url(create_post(editor, title=\"Older post\"))\n    create_post(editor, title=\"Newer post\")\n    act(admin, old, \"publish\")\n    page = admin.get(\"/admin/posts\").text\n    assert titles_in_order(page, [\"Older post\", \"Newer post\"]) == [\"Newer post\", \"Older post\"]\n    for label, n in ((\"All\", 2), (\"Published\", 1), (\"Draft\", 1)):\n        assert re.search(rf'<a href=\"[^\"]*status=\\w+[^\"]*\">{label} \\({n}\\)</a>', page), label\n\n\ndef test_status_filter_limits_the_list(client_as):\n    editor = client_as(\"editor\")\n    admin = president_for(editor)\n    live = post_url(create_post(editor, title=\"Live one\"))\n    create_post(editor, title=\"Draft one\")\n    act(admin, live, \"publish\")\n    published = admin.get(\"/admin/posts?status=published\").text\n    assert \"Live one\" in published and \"Draft one\" not in published\n    drafts = admin.get(\"/admin/posts?status=draft\").text\n    assert \"Draft one\" in drafts and \"Live one\" not in drafts\n    both = admin.get(\"/admin/posts?status=all\").text\n    assert \"Live one\" in both and \"Draft one\" in both\n    assert \"Live one\" in admin.get(\"/admin/posts?status=bogus\").text  # unknown means All\n\n\ndef test_type_filter_limits_to_posts_or_pages_without_breaking(client_as):\n    editor = client_as(\"editor\")\n    admin = president_for(editor)\n    create_post(editor, title=\"A post\")\n    assert \"A post\" in admin.get(\"/admin/posts?type=post\").text\n    pages = admin.get(\"/admin/posts?type=page\")\n    assert pages.status_code == 200\n    assert \"A post\" not in pages.text\n    assert admin.get(\"/admin/posts?type=bogus\").status_code == 200\n\n\ndef test_officer_list_and_counts_include_only_their_own_posts(client_as):\n    editor = client_as(\"editor\")\n    admin = president_for(editor)\n    create_post(editor, title=\"Mine\")\n    token_page = admin.get(\"/admin/posts/new\")\n    assert token_page.status_code == 200\n    create_post(admin, title=\"President only\")\n    listing = editor.get(\"/admin/posts\").text\n    assert \"Mine\" in listing and \"President only\" not in listing\n    assert count_of(editor.get(\"/admin\").text, \"Drafts\") == 1\n    assert count_of(admin.get(\"/admin\").text, \"Drafts\") == 2\n    assert re.search(r\">All \\(1\\)</a>\", listing)\n    assert \"President only\" not in editor.get(\"/admin/posts?status=draft\").text\nEOF\npython -m pytest tests/test_t04_dashboard.py -q 2>&1 | tail -15"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"container":null,"stop_reason":"tool_use","stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":2164,"cache_read_input_tokens":59946,"output_tokens":2073,"output_tokens_details":{"thinking_tokens":197},"server_tool_use":{"web_search_requests":0,"web_fetch_requests":0},"service_tier":"standard","cache_creation":{"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":2164,"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0},"inference_geo":"global","iterations":[{"input_tokens":2,"output_tokens":2073,"cache_read_input_tokens":59946,"cache_creation_input_tokens":2164,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":2164},"type":"message"}],"speed":"standard","fallback_credit":null},"input_transformations":[],"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"wireToolInputs":{"toolu_01HKE2NeuhtQRh2PGJMgjoiz":{"command":"cd Seam 1: the HTTP app.\"\"\"\nimport re\n\nfrom app import db\nfrom tests.test_t02_posts import create_post, post_url\nfrom tests.test_t03_president_control import act, president_for\n\n\ndef count_of(html: str, label: str) -> int:\n    \"\"\"The number printed next to a label such as 'Drafts: 3'.\"\"\"\n    return int(re.search(rf\"{label}:\\s*(\\d+)\", html).group(1))\n\n\ndef titles_in_order(html: str, titles: list[str]) -> list[str]:\n    return sorted((t for t in titles if t in html), key=html.index)\n\n\ndef test_dashboard_counts_follow_post_changes(client_as):\n    editor = client_as(\"editor\")\n    admin = president_for(editor)\n    assert count_of(admin.get(\"/admin\").text, \"Drafts\") == 0\n    url = post_url(create_post(editor, title=\"One\"))\n    create_post(editor, title=\"Two\")\n    page = admin.get(\"/admin\").text\n    assert (count_of(page, \"Drafts\"), count_of(page, \"Published\")) == (2, 0)\n    act(admin, url, \"publish\")\n    page = admin.get(\"/admin\").text\n    assert (count_of(page, \"Drafts\"), count_of(page, \"Published\")) == (1, 1)\n\n\ndef test_dashboard_has_a_new_post_button_that_opens_the_editor(client_as):\n    c = client_as(\"editor\")\n    page = c.get(\"/admin\").text\n    assert 'href=\"/admin/posts/new\"' in page\n    assert c.get(\"/admin/posts/new\").status_code == 200\n\n\ndef test_president_sees_user_counts_officer_does_not(client_as):\n    editor = client_as(\"editor\")\n    admin = president_for(editor)\n    page = admin.get(\"/admin\").text\n    assert (count_of(page, \"Active Users\"), count_of(page, \"Deactivated Users\")) == (2, 0)\n    conn = db.connect(editor.app.state.database)\n    with conn:\n        conn.execute(\"UPDATE users SET active = 0 WHERE role = 'editor'\")\n    conn.close()\n    page = admin.get(\"/admin\").text\n    assert (count_of(page, \"Active Users\"), count_of(page, \"Deactivated Users\")) == (1, 1)\n    assert \"Active Users\" not in editor.get(\"/admin\").text\n    assert \"Deactivated Users\" not in editor.get(\"/admin\").text\n\n\ndef test_content_list_is_newest_first_with_status_links_and_counts(client_as):\n    editor = client_as(\"editor\")\n    admin = president_for(editor)\n    old = post_url(create_post(editor, title=\"Older post\"))\n    create_post(editor, title=\"Newer post\")\n    act(admin, old, \"publish\")\n    page = admin.get(\"/admin/posts\").text\n    assert titles_in_order(page, [\"Older post\", \"Newer post\"]) == [\"Newer post\", \"Older post\"]\n    for label, n in ((\"All\", 2), (\"Published\", 1), (\"Draft\", 1)):\n        assert re.search(rf'<a href=\"[^\"]*status=\\w+[^\"]*\">{label} \\({n}\\)</a>', page), label\n\n\ndef test_status_filter_limits_the_list(client_as):\n    editor = client_as(\"editor\")\n    admin = president_for(editor)\n    live = post_url(create_post(editor, title=\"Live one\"))\n    create_post(editor, title=\"Draft one\")\n    act(admin, live, \"publish\")\n    published = admin.get(\"/admin/posts?status=published\").text\n    assert \"Live one\" in published and \"Draft one\" not in published\n    drafts = admin.get(\"/admin/posts?status=draft\").text\n    assert \"Draft one\" in drafts and \"Live one\" not in drafts\n    both = admin.get(\"/admin/posts?status=all\").text\n    assert \"Live one\" in both and \"Draft one\" in both\n    assert \"Live one\" in admin.get(\"/admin/posts?status=bogus\").text  # unknown means All\n\n\ndef test_type_filter_limits_to_posts_or_pages_without_breaking(client_as):\n    editor = client_as(\"editor\")\n    admin = president_for(editor)\n    create_post(editor, title=\"A post\")\n    assert \"A post\" in admin.get(\"/admin/posts?type=post\").text\n    pages = admin.get(\"/admin/posts?type=page\")\n    assert pages.status_code == 200\n    assert \"A post\" not in pages.text\n    assert admin.get(\"/admin/posts?type=bogus\").status_code == 200\n\n\ndef test_officer_list_and_counts_include_only_their_own_posts(client_as):\n    editor = client_as(\"editor\")\n    admin = president_for(editor)\n    create_post(editor, title=\"Mine\")\n    token_page = admin.get(\"/admin/posts/new\")\n    assert token_page.status_code == 200\n    create_post(admin, title=\"President only\")\n    listing = editor.get(\"/admin/posts\").text\n    assert \"Mine\" in listing and \"President only\" not in listing\n    assert count_of(editor.get(\"/admin\").text, \"Drafts\") == 1\n    assert count_of(admin.get(\"/admin\").text, \"Drafts\") == 2\n    assert re.search(r\">All \\(1\\)</a>\", listing)\n    assert \"President only\" not in editor.get(\"/admin/posts?status=draft\").text\nEOF\npython -m pytest tests/test_t04_dashboard.py -q 2>&1 | tail -15"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}]}}

{"type":"api-request-blob","sessionId":"ead3cae9-0b68-4987-b0da-e9d5763f22e8","hash":"22ec0738d38c741e1e09bf8fefb801f36a40ca33775dbdb3e20adfce76929e59","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"type":"tool_result","tool_use_id":"toolu_014pjkz7V87gRn7rxdUtoKut","content":"Launching skill: code-review"},{"type":"text","text":"Base directory for this skill: /Users/biswas1/code/iphs400-mp2-cms/.claude/skills/code-review\n\nTwo-axis review of the diff between `HEAD` and a fixed point the user supplies:\n\n- **Standards**: does the code conform to this repo's documented coding standards?\n- **Spec**: does the code faithfully implement the originating issue / spec?\n\nBoth axes run as **parallel sub-agents** so they don't pollute each other's context, then this skill aggregates their findings.\n\nThe issue tracker should have been provided to you. If not, tell the user to run `/setup-matt-pocock-skills`.\n\n## Process\n\n### 1. Pin the fixed point\n\nWhatever the user said is the fixed point (a commit SHA, branch name, tag, `main`, `HEAD~5`, etc.). If they didn't specify one, ask for it.\n\nCapture the diff command once: `git diff <fixed-point>...HEAD` (three-dot, so the comparison is against the merge-base). Also note the list of commits via `git log <fixed-point>..HEAD --oneline`.\n\nBefore going further, confirm the fixed point resolves (`git rev-parse <fixed-point>`) and the diff is non-empty. A bad ref or empty diff should fail here, not inside two parallel sub-agents.\n\n### 2. Identify the spec source\n\nLook for the originating spec, in this order:\n\n1. Issue references in the commit messages (`#123`, `Closes #45`, GitLab `!67`, etc.), fetched via the workflow in the tracker doc.\n2. A path the user passed as an argument.\n3. A spec file under `docs/`, `specs/`, or `.scratch/` matching the branch name or feature.\n4. If nothing is found, ask the user where the spec is. If they say there isn't one, the **Spec** sub-agent will skip and report \"no spec available\".\n\n### 3. Identify the standards sources\n\nSearch the repo for every file that documents how code should be written. When `CODING_STANDARDS.md` or `CONTRIBUTING.md` exists, it must be on the list.\n\nOn top of whatever the repo documents, the Standards axis always carries the **smell baseline** below: a fixed set of Fowler code smells (_Refactoring_, ch.3) that applies even when a repo documents nothing. Two rules bind it:\n\n- **The repo overrides.** A documented repo standard always wins; where it endorses something the baseline would flag, suppress the smell.\n- **Always a judgement call.** Each smell is a labelled heuristic (\"possible Feature Envy\"), never a hard violation. Like any standard here, skip anything tooling already enforces.\n\nEach smell reads *what it is* → *how to fix*; match it against the diff:\n\n- **Mysterious Name**: a function, variable, or type whose name doesn't reveal what it does or holds. → rename it; if no honest name comes, the design's murky.\n- **Duplicated Code**: the same logic shape appears in more than one hunk or file in the change. → extract the shared shape, call it from both.\n- **Feature Envy**: a method that reaches into another object's data more than its own. → move the method onto the data it envies.\n- **Data Clumps**: the same few fields or params keep travelling together (a type wanting to be born). → bundle them into one type, pass that.\n- **Primitive Obsession**: a primitive or string standing in for a domain concept that deserves its own type. → give the concept its own small type.\n- **Repeated Switches**: the same `switch`/`if`-cascade on the same type recurs across the change. → replace with polymorphism, or one map both sites share.\n- **Shotgun Surgery**: one logical change forces scattered edits across many files in the diff. → gather what changes together into one module.\n- **Divergent Change**: one file or module is edited for several unrelated reasons. → split so each module changes for one reason.\n- **Speculative Generality**: abstraction, parameters, or hooks added for needs the spec doesn't have. → delete it; inline back until a real need shows.\n- **Message Chains**: long `a.b().c().d()` navigation the caller shouldn't depend on. → hide the walk behind one method on the first object.\n- **Middle Man**: a class or function that mostly just delegates onward. → cut it, call the real target direct.\n- **Refused Bequest**: a subclass or implementer that ignores or overrides most of what it inherits. → drop the inheritance, use composition.\n\n### 4. Spawn both sub-agents in parallel\n\nIssue both sub-agent calls together, in the foreground, and aggregate the reports they return.\n\n**Standards sub-agent prompt** should include:\n\n- The full diff command and commit list.\n- The list of standards-source files you found in step 3, **plus the smell baseline from step 3** pasted in full (the sub-agent has no other access to it).\n- The brief: \"Report, per file/hunk where relevant, (a) every place the diff violates a documented standard: cite the standard (file + the rule); and (b) any baseline smell you spot: name it and quote the hunk. Distinguish hard violations from judgement calls: documented-standard breaches can be hard, but baseline smells are always judgement calls, and a documented repo standard overrides the baseline. Skip anything tooling enforces. Under 400 words.\"\n\n**Spec sub-agent prompt** should include:\n\n- The diff command and commit list.\n- The path or fetched contents of the spec.\n- The brief: \"Report: (a) requirements the spec asked for that are missing or partial; (b) behaviour in the diff that wasn't asked for (scope creep); (c) requirements that look implemented but where the implementation looks wrong. Quote the spec line for each finding. Under 400 words.\"\n\nIf the spec is missing, skip the Spec sub-agent and note this in the final report.\n\n### 5. Aggregate\n\nPresent the two reports under `## Standards` and `## Spec` headings, verbatim or lightly cleaned. Do **not** merge or rerank findings, because the two axes are deliberately separate (see _Why two axes_).\n\nEnd with a one-line summary: total findings per axis, and the worst issue _within each axis_ (if any). Don't pick a single winner across axes: that's the reranking the separation exists to prevent.\n\n## Why two axes\n\nA change can pass one axis and fail the other:\n\n- Code that follows every standard but implements the wrong thing → **Standards pass, Spec fail.**\n- Code that does exactly what the issue asked but breaks the project's conventions → **Spec pass, Standards fail.**\n\nReporting them separately stops one axis from masking the other.\n\n\nARGUMENTS: uncommitted changes since HEAD for ticket #5 (T04)"}]}}

{"parentUuid":"2e9c9fae-6e26-4c3b-9fc2-997ca326101b","isSidechain":false,"promptId":"cb145c49-8cdc-415f-98a5-dd675de1cd2b","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"type":"text","text":"Base directory for this skill: /Users/biswas1/code/iphs400-mp2-cms/.claude/skills/code-review\n\nTwo-axis review of the diff between `HEAD` and a fixed point the user supplies:\n\n- **Standards**: does the code conform to this repo's documented coding standards?\n- **Spec**: does the code faithfully implement the originating issue / spec?\n\nBoth axes run as **parallel sub-agents** so they don't pollute each other's context, then this skill aggregates their findings.\n\nThe issue tracker should have been provided to you. If not, tell the user to run `/setup-matt-pocock-skills`.\n\n## Process\n\n### 1. Pin the fixed point\n\nWhatever the user said is the fixed point (a commit SHA, branch name, tag, `main`, `HEAD~5`, etc.). If they didn't specify one, ask for it.\n\nCapture the diff command once: `git diff <fixed-point>...HEAD` (three-dot, so the comparison is against the merge-base). Also note the list of commits via `git log <fixed-point>..HEAD --oneline`.\n\nBefore going further, confirm the fixed point resolves (`git rev-parse <fixed-point>`) and the diff is non-empty. A bad ref or empty diff should fail here, not inside two parallel sub-agents.\n\n### 2. Identify the spec source\n\nLook for the originating spec, in this order:\n\n1. Issue references in the commit messages (`#123`, `Closes #45`, GitLab `!67`, etc.), fetched via the workflow in the tracker doc.\n2. A path the user passed as an argument.\n3. A spec file under `docs/`, `specs/`, or `.scratch/` matching the branch name or feature.\n4. If nothing is found, ask the user where the spec is. If they say there isn't one, the **Spec** sub-agent will skip and report \"no spec available\".\n\n### 3. Identify the standards sources\n\nSearch the repo for every file that documents how code should be written. When `CODING_STANDARDS.md` or `CONTRIBUTING.md` exists, it must be on the list.\n\nOn top of whatever the repo documents, the Standards axis always carries the **smell baseline** below: a fixed set of Fowler code smells (_Refactoring_, ch.3) that applies even when a repo documents nothing. Two rules bind it:\n\n- **The repo overrides.** A documented repo standard always wins; where it endorses something the baseline would flag, suppress the smell.\n- **Always a judgement call.** Each smell is a labelled heuristic (\"possible Feature Envy\"), never a hard violation. Like any standard here, skip anything tooling already enforces.\n\nEach smell reads *what it is* → *how to fix*; match it against the diff:\n\n- **Mysterious Name**: a function, variable, or type whose name doesn't reveal what it does or holds. → rename it; if no honest name comes, the design's murky.\n- **Duplicated Code**: the same logic shape appears in more than one hunk or file in the change. → extract the shared shape, call it from both.\n- **Feature Envy**: a method that reaches into another object's data more than its own. → move the method onto the data it envies.\n- **Data Clumps**: the same few fields or params keep travelling together (a type wanting to be born). → bundle them into one type, pass that.\n- **Primitive Obsession**: a primitive or string standing in for a domain concept that deserves its own type. → give the concept its own small type.\n- **Repeated Switches**: the same `switch`/`if`-cascade on the same type recurs across the change. → replace with polymorphism, or one map both sites share.\n- **Shotgun Surgery**: one logical change forces scattered edits across many files in the diff. → gather what changes together into one module.\n- **Divergent Change**: one file or module is edited for several unrelated reasons. → split so each module changes for one reason.\n- **Speculative Generality**: abstraction, parameters, or hooks added for needs the spec doesn't have. → delete it; inline back until a real need shows.\n- **Message Chains**: long `a.b().c().d()` navigation the caller shouldn't depend on. → hide the walk behind one method on the first object.\n- **Middle Man**: a class or function that mostly just delegates onward. → cut it, call the real target direct.\n- **Refused Bequest**: a subclass or implementer that ignores or overrides most of what it inherits. → drop the inheritance, use composition.\n\n### 4. Spawn both sub-agents in parallel\n\nIssue both sub-agent calls together, in the foreground, and aggregate the reports they return.\n\n**Standards sub-agent prompt** should include:\n\n- The full diff command and commit list.\n- The list of standards-source files you found in step 3, **plus the smell baseline from step 3** pasted in full (the sub-agent has no other access to it).\n- The brief: \"Report, per file/hunk where relevant, (a) every place the diff violates a documented standard: cite the standard (file + the rule); and (b) any baseline smell you spot: name it and quote the hunk. Distinguish hard violations from judgement calls: documented-standard breaches can be hard, but baseline smells are always judgement calls, and a documented repo standard overrides the baseline. Skip anything tooling enforces. Under 400 words.\"\n\n**Spec sub-agent prompt** should include:\n\n- The diff command and commit list.\n- The path or fetched contents of the spec.\n- The brief: \"Report: (a) requirements the spec asked for that are missing or partial; (b) behaviour in the diff that wasn't asked for (scope creep); (c) requirements that look implemented but where the implementation looks wrong. Quote the spec line for each finding. Under 400 words.\"\n\nIf the spec is missing, skip the Spec sub-agent and note this in the final report.\n\n### 5. Aggregate\n\nPresent the two reports under `## Standards` and `## Spec` headings, verbatim or lightly cleaned. Do **not** merge or rerank findings, because the two axes are deliberately separate (see _Why two axes_).\n\nEnd with a one-line summary: total findings per axis, and the worst issue _within each axis_ (if any). Don't pick a single winner across axes: that's the reranking the separation exists to prevent.\n\n## Why two axes\n\nA change can pass one axis and fail the other:\n\n- Code that follows every standard but implements the wrong thing → **Standards pass, Spec fail.**\n- Code that does exactly what the issue asked but breaks the project's conventions → **Spec pass, Standards fail.**\n\nReporting them separately stops one axis from masking the other.\n\n\nARGUMENTS: uncommitted changes since HEAD for ticket #5 (T04)"}]},"isMeta":true,"turnCompanion":true,"uuid":"216b8c40-85e1-4bf3-9dfb-b6592e242b96","timestamp":"2026-10-15T03:53:15.584Z","sourceToolUseID":"toolu_014pjkz7V87gRn7rxdUtoKut","session_id":"ead3cae9-0b68-4987-b0da-e9d5763f22e8","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/biswas1/code/iphs400-mp2-cms","sessionId":"ead3cae9-0b68-4987-b0da-e9d5763f22e8","version":"2.1.300","gitBranch":"main"}

The user can send it another message and resume it, so the same task-id may notify more than once.</note>\n<result>**Review of uncommitted T04 work (ticket #5)**\n\n**(a) Documented-standard breaches**\n\n1. `templates/admin/hello.html` and `app/routes/admin.py` break the CONTEXT.md **Dashboard** entry. That entry says the Dashboard shows Drafts, Published **and Pending changes**. The diff has no Pending changes count. Pending changes are described as shown in a banner, and no banner exists either. This is the clearest breach.\n2. `app/routes/posts.py` (`TYPE_FILTERS = (\"all\", \"post\", \"page\")`) and `post_list.html` (Type nav) break the CONTEXT.md **Content list** entry, \"Editors see Posts only\". Officers are offered a \"Page\" filter. It is harmless today, because it returns an empty list. It also breaks the Editor definition (\"cannot touch Pages\"). The Type nav should be Admin-only.\n3. `post_list.html` has `&lt;h1&gt;Content list&lt;/h1&gt;`, but the route still passes `\"title\": \"Posts\"`. This is a small inconsistency with the one-word-per-concept rule in `docs/agents/domain.md`. The page names the same concept two ways.\n4. No other CLAUDE.md, SECURITY-CHECKLIST.md or docs/agents rule is breached.\n   - Access control (#4, #10) is enforced in the route: user counts are computed only for admins.\n   - Query values are whitelisted, and the template autoescapes them.\n   - The \"relative paths only\" rule applies to published HTML, not admin templates.\n\n**(b) Baseline smells (judgement calls)**\n\n- **Mysterious Name** (`app/routes/posts.py`): `def post_list(request, status: str = \"all\", type: str = \"all\", ...)`. `type` shadows a builtin and is reused as both parameter and local.\n  - In `tests/test_t04_dashboard.py`, `act(admin, url, \"publish\")` and `president_for(editor)` hide what they do. They are imported from another test module.\n- **Duplicated Code**:\n  - The `author_id` `WHERE` building appears in both `list_posts` and `count_by_status` (`app/posts.py`).\n  - The scoping line `None if is_admin else user.id` (`admin.py`) repeats `own_only = None if user.role == \"admin\" else user.id` (`posts.py`).\n- **Primitive Obsession**:\n  - `user.role == \"admin\"` is a bare string, repeated.\n  - Status keys `\"all\"/\"published\"/\"draft\"` are strings mapped through `STATUS_FILTERS`.\n  - `status_links` is a list of `(key, label, n)` tuples.\n  - `count_by_active` returns a bare `(active, deactivated)` tuple whose order is easy to swap.\n- **Data Clumps**: `drafts`, `published`, `active_users` and `deactivated_users` are passed as four loose context values. `status, type` and `status_links, type_links` travel together.\n- **Feature Envy / Divergent Change** (`post_list`): the route assembles view-model data (`published + drafts`, link tuples, the page-stub branch). That logic belongs with the posts module or a helper. The route now changes for filters, counts and the Pages stub alike.\n- **Speculative Generality**:\n  - `shows_posts` and `counts.get(..., 0)` with a `{}` fallback exist only for the unwritten Pages feature (\"Pages arrive in T05\").\n  - `type_links` is generic though only one type exists. The Type filter is in the CONTEXT.md Content list entry, so this is acceptable if the ticket asked for it.\n- **Test smell**: `test_officer_list_and_counts_include_only_their_own_posts` does `token_page = admin.get(\"/admin/posts/new\")` and asserts its status. This is dead setup that tests nothing about the ticket.\n- **Shotgun Surgery (minor)**: renaming \"Posts\" to \"Content list\" touched the h1, the dashboard link and the empty-state text. Only the h1 and link were updated, and the route title was missed (see 3).\n\nI made no changes to any files.</result>\n<usage><subagent_tokens>46412</subagent_tokens><tool_uses>3</tool_uses><duration_ms>23708</duration_ms></usage>\n</task-notification>"}

This is the clearest breach.\n2. `app/routes/posts.py` (`TYPE_FILTERS = (\"all\", \"post\", \"page\")`) and `post_list.html` (Type nav) break the CONTEXT.md **Content list** entry, \"Editors see Posts only\". Officers are offered a \"Page\" filter. It is harmless today, because it returns an empty list. It also breaks the Editor definition (\"cannot touch Pages\"). The Type nav should be Admin-only.\n3. `post_list.html` has `&lt;h1&gt;Content list&lt;/h1&gt;`, but the route still passes `\"title\": \"Posts\"`. This is a small inconsistency with the one-word-per-concept rule in `docs/agents/domain.md`. The page names the same concept two ways.\n4. No other CLAUDE.md, SECURITY-CHECKLIST.md or docs/agents rule is breached.\n   - Access control (#4, #10) is enforced in the route: user counts are computed only for admins.\n   - Query values are whitelisted, and the template autoescapes them.\n   - The \"relative paths only\" rule applies to published HTML, not admin templates.\n\n**(b) Baseline smells (judgement calls)**\n\n- **Mysterious Name** (`app/routes/posts.py`): `def post_list(request, status: str = \"all\", type: str = \"all\", ...)`. `type` shadows a builtin and is reused as both parameter and local.\n  - In `tests/test_t04_dashboard.py`, `act(admin, url, \"publish\")` and `president_for(editor)` hide what they do. They are imported from another test module.\n- **Duplicated Code**:\n  - The `author_id` `WHERE` building appears in both `list_posts` and `count_by_status` (`app/posts.py`).\n  - The scoping line `None if is_admin else user.id` (`admin.py`) repeats `own_only = None if user.role == \"admin\" else user.id` (`posts.py`).\n- **Primitive Obsession**:\n  - `user.role == \"admin\"` is a bare string, repeated.\n  - Status keys `\"all\"/\"published\"/\"draft\"` are strings mapped through `STATUS_FILTERS`.\n  - `status_links` is a list of `(key, label, n)` tuples.\n  - `count_by_active` returns a bare `(active, deactivated)` tuple whose order is easy to swap.\n- **Data Clumps**: `drafts`, `published`, `active_users` and `deactivated_users` are passed as four loose context values. `status, type` and `status_links, type_links` travel together.\n- **Feature Envy / Divergent Change** (`post_list`): the route assembles view-model data (`published + drafts`, link tuples, the page-stub branch). That logic belongs with the posts module or a helper. The route now changes for filters, counts and the Pages stub alike.\n- **Speculative Generality**:\n  - `shows_posts` and `counts.get(..., 0)` with a `{}` fallback exist only for the unwritten Pages feature (\"Pages arrive in T05\").\n  - `type_links` is generic though only one type exists. The Type filter is in the CONTEXT.md Content list entry, so this is acceptable if the ticket asked for it.\n- **Test smell**: `test_officer_list_and_counts_include_only_their_own_posts` does `token_page = admin.get(\"/admin/posts/new\")` and asserts its status. This is dead setup that tests nothing about the ticket.\n- **Shotgun Surgery (minor)**: renaming \"Posts\" to \"Content list\" touched the h1, the dashboard link and the empty-state text. Only the h1 and link were updated, and the route title was missed (see 3).\n\nI made no changes to any files.</result>\n<usage><subagent_tokens>46412</subagent_tokens><tool_uses>3</tool_uses><duration_ms>23708</duration_ms></usage>\n</task-notification>"},"uuid":"b199c1ca-4da3-407d-9b22-02270f35f4a0","timestamp":"2026-10-15T03:53:44.112Z","permissionMode":"bypassPermissions","origin":{"kind":"task-notification","producer":"session-task","runId":"0mv907c40-9c45ac04"},"promptSource":"system","turnOrigin":"task_notification","turnPosition":{"promptIndex":1,"turnIndex":2},"queueSkipAttachments":true,"userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/biswas1/code/iphs400-mp2-cms","sessionId":"ead3cae9-0b68-4987-b0da-e9d5763f22e8","version":"2.1.300","gitBranch":"main"}
