The user can send it another message and resume it, so the same task-id may notify more than once.</note>\n<result>**Review of uncommitted T04 work against issue #5 and spec #1.** I read the diff and the tests but did not run them.\n\n**(a) Missing or partial**\n- **Tests for both roles.** Issue #5: \"Tests cover counts and filters for both roles via `client_as`\". Admin gets the full set of tests. The Officer gets one test (`test_officer_list_and_counts_include_only_their_own_posts`) and it is thin:\n  - It checks only the Draft count, `All (1)` and `status=draft`.\n  - It never checks the Officer's Published count, the Published or All filter, or the type filter.\n  - The Officer's Published count matters because an Officer's own Post can be Published.\n- **Pages in the counts.** Spec #1 item 5: \"counts of Drafts, Published items\". Only Posts are counted, so Pages are ignored. That is acceptable until T05, but it is a gap to track.\n- **Commit and review steps.** Issue #5: \"Commit starts 'T04:' and ends 'Closes #5'; `/code-review` findings are posted on this issue first\". Not done yet, which is expected for uncommitted work.\n- **Pending-changes banner.** Issue #5 says: \"The Pending-changes banner is added in T07\". Correctly left out.\n\n**(b) Not asked for**\n- **Template wording.** `post_list.html` heading changed from \"Posts\" to \"Content list\", and the empty-state text changed from \"No Posts yet.\" to \"Nothing here yet.\" `hello.html` gained a new `&lt;h2&gt;Dashboard&lt;/h2&gt;`, and its \"Posts\" link is now \"Content list\". None of this is in the issue.\n- **Unknown filter values.** The code falls back to All for `status=bogus` and `type=bogus`. The issue says nothing about this, and a test locks it in. It is harmless but unrequested.\n- **Stray ledger change.** `notes/usage-ledger.csv` is modified. It is a hook artifact, not T04 work, so it should not go in the \"T04:\" commit unless that is your usual practice.\n- **Dead test code.** In `test_officer_list_and_counts_include_only_their_own_posts`, the `token_page = admin.get(\"/admin/posts/new\")` line and its assertion do nothing.\n\n**(c) Looks implemented but may be wrong**\n- **Type filter and status counts.** Issue #5: \"A type filter limits the list to Posts or Pages (…the filter must not break before then)\".\n  - With `type=page`, the code skips the Post queries, so every status link shows `(0)`. That is correct today.\n  - It is hard-coded, not a real filter. When T05 lands, `shows_posts` and the empty `counts` will have to be rewritten.\n  - The type links carry no counts, so the status counts do not follow a \"type\" model. Spec #1 only asks for status counts, so this is minor.\n- **Newest-first ordering.** Issue #5: \"Content list is newest first\". The list is ordered by `created_at DESC, id DESC`. If \"newest\" means most recently updated, this is wrong. The spec is ambiguous, so confirm with the user. The test only covers creation order.\n- **Editor access to the type filter.** Spec #1 item 2: \"cannot touch any Page\". Editors can request `?type=page`. It returns an empty list with a 200, which is safe because no Page data is queried. When T05 adds Pages, this route must filter them out for Editors. Nothing enforces that now, and no test guards it.\n- **User counts.** `count_by_active` uses `SUM(active)`, which assumes the `active` column only ever holds 0 or 1. The tests do exercise Active and Deactivated counts, so it looks fine.</result>\n<usage><subagent_tokens>47655</subagent_tokens><tool_uses>4</tool_uses><duration_ms>45072</duration_ms></usage>\n</task-notification>"}

{"parentUuid":"ad34dfab-790e-400b-ae5c-f2ddc22f5306","isSidechain":false,"message":{"model":"claude-sonnet-5-5","id":"msg_011Cg3M6V8k1mmFbQSepSRVA","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"T04 (#5, Dashboard counts and filterable Content list) is committed and pushed to `main` as `4a11b75`. The full suite passes, 75 tests. I didn't run `ruff` because it isn't installed in the project environment. I didn't start the server to check the pages by hand, so the dashboard and Content list are verified only through the tests.\n\n- **Implementation:** the dashboard shows Draft and Published counts and a \"New Post\" button. Presidents also see active and Deactivated User counts. The Content list is newest first, with All / Published / Draft links that each show a count, plus a Post / Page type filter. Officers see only their own Posts and counts.\n- **Page filter:** `type=page` returns an empty list until T05 adds Pages.\n- **Tests:** `tests/test_t04_dashboard.py` covers counts and filters for both roles through `client_as`.\n- **Code-review:** I posted the findings and resolutions on issue #5. I fixed these:\n  - Editors no longer see the Page filter.\n  - The page title is now \"Content list\".\n  - The `type` parameter is renamed `kind`.\n  - The Officer test is stronger.\n- **Review items I left alone:** the small code smells and the missing Pending changes count, which belongs to T07.\n- **Ledger:** I left `notes/usage-ledger.csv` out of the commit, so it is still modified in the working tree.
